<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-49563355" data-id="49563355" data-tags="AI|Science" data-score="2256" data-comments="1574" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49563355" data-time="1788522893">
<a class="thumb" href="https://collusion.wiki/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://collusion.wiki/img/social.png?v=5ce9724f' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='collusion.wiki' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://collusion.wiki/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Discovery of a new OpenAI agent message board</span><span class='zh'>发现一个新的 OpenAI 代理消息板</span></a><span class="dom">collusion.wiki</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new OpenAI agent message board has been discovered, suggesting a hidden or internal communication channel used by agent simulations.</span><span class='zh'>发现了一个新的 OpenAI 代理消息板，表明在代理模拟中可能存在未公开的沟通渠道。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A new OpenAI agent message board has been discovered, suggesting a hidden or internal communication channel used by agent simulations. The finding raises questions about agent coordination, safety, and monitoring of emergent behaviours. It underscores how researchers trace and audit agent interactions as AI systems scale.</span><span class='zh'>发现了一个新的 OpenAI 代理消息板，表明在代理模拟中可能存在未公开的沟通渠道。此发现引发对代理协作、安全以及对新兴行为监控的担忧。也凸显了在 AI 规模化运行中追踪和审计代理互动的重要性。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>2256</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49563355' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1574</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-49554643" data-id="49554643" data-tags="AI" data-score="2234" data-comments="2046" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49554643" data-time="1788460865">
<a class="thumb" href="https://openai.com/index/gpt-6-astra/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/6ibCaqsoO7F6XCNCIO8zaZ/9475cdebc1f0af100414f1c85860fc4c/Hero_16x9.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='openai.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/gpt-6-astra/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GPT-6 Astra</span><span class='zh'>GPT-6 Astra</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI introduces GPT-6 Astra, signaling a leap in the next-gen language model family.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 推出 GPT-6 Astra，标志着下一代语言模型的跃进。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI introduces GPT-6 Astra, signaling a leap in the next-gen language model family. The announcement hints at expanded reasoning, safety controls, and deployment options aimed at enterprise-scale AI workloads. For developers and researchers, Astra&#x27;s release could shift expectations around performance and compliance in AI tooling.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 推出 GPT-6 Astra，标志着下一代语言模型的跃进。公告暗示在推理能力、安全控制和面向企业的部署选项方面的改进，可能改变开发者对性能与合规性的预期。对于开发者和研究者而言，这一发布将影响 AI 工具的性能与合规性基线。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/claude-fable-and-mythos-5-1" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Fable 5.1 and Claude Mythos 5.1</span><span class='zh'>Claude Fable 5.1 与 Claude Mythos 5.1</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic released Claude Fable 5.1 and Claude Mythos 5.1, minor updates to its Claude family.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 发布 Claude Fable 5.1 与 Claude Mythos 5.1，强调安全性、可靠性和改进的工具使用能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic released Claude Fable 5.1 and Claude Mythos 5.1, minor updates to its Claude family. The changes focus on safety, reliability, and improved tool-using capabilities in chat and coding workflows, which may influence how developers integrate Claude into apps. Users should test for behavior changes, prompt handling tweaks, and any impacts on latency or pricing.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 发布 Claude Fable 5.1 与 Claude Mythos 5.1，强调安全性、可靠性和改进的工具使用能力。此举可能影响开发者在构建基于 Claude 的应用时的提示设计、令牌消耗与延迟。请尽早评估新版本的行为变化。</span></p>

The update aims to empower more responsive AI applications and better developer tooling, reinforcing Google&#x27;s push to compete with other high-end LLMs.</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 3.8 Flash 是谷歌 Gemini 系列的最新版本，承诺提升推理速度与实时交互的流畅度。该更新旨在为更快速的 AI 应用和更完善的开发工具提供支持，凸显谷歌在高端大模型领域的竞争策略。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/audacity/audacity/releases/tag/Audacity-4.0.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Audacity 4.0</span><span class='zh'>Audacity 4.0 发布</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Audacity 4.0 marks a major milestone for the popular open-source audio editor.</span><span class='zh'>Audacity 4.0 是这个开源音频编辑器的最新重大版本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Audacity 4.0 marks a major milestone for the popular open-source audio editor. It focuses on stability and ongoing maintenance, with improvements to performance and compatibility for modern formats and plugins.</span><span class='zh'>Audacity 4.0 是这个开源音频编辑器的最新重大版本。它聚焦稳定性与持续维护，带来性能优化和对现代音频格式及插件的兼容性改进。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.newsonaut.com/articles/hang-on-to-your-firefox" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hang on to Your Firefox</span><span class='zh'>坚持使用你的 Firefox</span></a><span class="dom">newsonaut.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Firefox continues to offer strong privacy protections and a modular extension ecosystem at a time of rapid browser changes.</span><span class='zh'>Firefox 仍然是注重隐私的用户的可行选择，浏览器生态在快速演进。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Firefox continues to offer strong privacy protections and a modular extension ecosystem at a time of rapid browser changes. The piece argues that users don’t have to switch to alternative engines to get modern features, emphasizing Mozilla’s open web commitments and ongoing performance improvements. It also provides practical tips for getting the most out of Firefox.</span><span class='zh'>Firefox 仍然是注重隐私的用户的可行选择，浏览器生态在快速演进。文章强调无需放弃 Firefox 就能获得现代功能，突出 Mozilla 的开放网络承诺与持续的性能改进。文中还给出如何最大化使用 Firefox 的实用建议。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">10</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ankidroid/Anki-Android/issues/21656" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AnkiDroid: Google Play no longer allowing Open Collective donation link</span><span class='zh'>AnkiDroid：Google Play 不再允许 Open Collective 捐赠链接</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google Play no longer allows apps to link to external donation pages such as Open Collective, forcing AnkiDroid to remove its donation link.</span><span class='zh'>Google Play 现在不再允许应用链接到外部捐赠页面，AnkiDroid 团队因此移除了 Open Collective 的捐赠链接。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google Play no longer allows apps to link to external donation pages such as Open Collective, forcing AnkiDroid to remove its donation link. This policy shift illustrates how app-store rules can constrain OSS funding models and push developers toward alternative monetization or sponsorship approaches. For a long-running open-source Android project, sustaining funding remains a critical challenge.</span><span class='zh'>Google Play 现在不再允许应用链接到外部捐赠页面，AnkiDroid 团队因此移除了 Open Collective 的捐赠链接。这一政策变化凸显了应用商店规则对开源资金模式的制约，可能促使开发者转向应用内购买或平台赞助等替代模式。对于长期运营的开源 Android 项目来说，资金筹措仍然是关键挑战。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>897</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49520022' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>272</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49526069" data-id="49526069" data-tags="AI" data-score="858" data-comments="1033" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49526069" data-time="1788287715">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://danluu.com/zitron/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How accurate have Ed Zitron&#x27;s AI skeptic predictions been?</span><span class='zh'>艾德·齐特龙的 AI 怀疑论预测有多准确？</span></a><span class="dom">danluu.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A retrospective assessment evaluates how accurate Ed Zitron&#x27;s AI-skeptic predictions have been, comparing past forecasts with now-established AI realities.</span><span class='zh'>本文回顾性评估知名公关人士 Ed Zitron 对 AI 的怀疑性预测的准确性，将其以往的判断与当前 AI 现实进行对比，揭示科技预测中的规律与偏差，并提出对读者的启示。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A retrospective assessment evaluates how accurate Ed Zitron&#x27;s AI-skeptic predictions have been, comparing past forecasts with now-established AI realities. It highlights patterns in tech prognostication and biases, offering takeaways about forecasting in tech journalism.</span><span class='zh'>本文回顾性评估知名公关人士 Ed Zitron 对 AI 的怀疑性预测的准确性，将其以往的判断与当前 AI 现实进行对比，揭示科技预测中的规律与偏差，并提出对读者的启示。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">12</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://fastpotify.rocks/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Fastpotify</span><span class='zh'>Fastpotify 极速版</span></a><span class="dom">fastpotify.rocks</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Fastpotify promises a leaner, faster Spotify-like experience, focusing on lower latency and a smaller client footprint.</span><span class='zh'>Fastpotify 提出更轻量、快速的 Spotify 风格体验，聚焦更低的延迟和更小的客户端体积。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Fastpotify promises a leaner, faster Spotify-like experience, focusing on lower latency and a smaller client footprint. If it delivers, it could push streaming tooling toward lighter, more open architectures and raise questions about compatibility and licensing.</span><span class='zh'>Fastpotify 提出更轻量、快速的 Spotify 风格体验，聚焦更低的延迟和更小的客户端体积。如果落地，它可能推动流媒体工具走向更开放、轻量化的架构，并带来兼容性与许可方面的讨论。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/cve-2026-85046" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=nvd.nist.gov&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>nvd.nist.gov</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">13</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/cve-2026-85046" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Actively exploited sandbox RCE in all Chromium versions</span><span class='zh'>所有 Chromium 版本均被主动利用的沙箱远程代码执行漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">nvd.nist.gov</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A sandbox escape vulnerability in Chromium has been actively exploited across all versions, enabling attackers to break out of the browser&#x27;s sandbox and run arbitrary code.</span><span class='zh'>Chromium 的沙箱逃逸漏洞正在被主动利用，影响所有版本，使攻击者能够突破沙箱执行任意代码。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A sandbox escape vulnerability in Chromium has been actively exploited across all versions, enabling attackers to break out of the browser&#x27;s sandbox and run arbitrary code. The broad impact across Chromium-based browsers makes timely patching critical; users should update to the latest build and apply recommended mitigations as they become available.</span><span class='zh'>Chromium 的沙箱逃逸漏洞正在被主动利用，影响所有版本，使攻击者能够突破沙箱执行任意代码。由于受影响的浏览器生态广泛，及时打补丁至关重要，用户应尽快更新到最新版本并遵循厂商的缓解建议。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>785</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49570669' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>478</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49568506" data-id="49568506" data-tags="Science" data-score="759" data-comments="494" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49568506" data-time="1788547376">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.anthropic.com/research/formalizing-fermats-last-theorem" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/578f91575c42231f0994e341014614255149af80-1200x630.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='anthropic.com' data-c='#10b981' data-tag='Science'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">14</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/research/formalizing-fermats-last-theorem" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Formalizing Fermat&#x27;s Last Theorem</span><span class='zh'>费马大定理的形式化</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers formalize Fermat&#x27;s Last Theorem in a proof assistant, producing a machine-checked verification of the centuries-old result.</span><span class='zh'>研究者在证明助手中对费马大定理进行了形式化，并获得机器可检验的证明。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers formalize Fermat&#x27;s Last Theorem in a proof assistant, producing a machine-checked verification of the centuries-old result. The effort demonstrates the maturing capability of formal methods to capture famous math proofs and hints at broader tooling that could aid verification of complex theorems. It signals potential for cross-pollination between pure mathematics and software verification.</span><span class='zh'>研究者在证明助手中对费马大定理进行了形式化，并获得机器可检验的证明。此举体现了形式化方法在验证经典数学结论方面的成熟度，并暗示其在未来支持更复杂定理的潜力。它也展示了数学与软件验证工具之间日益紧密的协作前景。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>759</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49568506' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>494</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49514878" data-id="49514878" data-tags="Web|Privacy" data-score="754" data-comments="606" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49514878" data-time="1788210658">
<a class="thumb" href="https://webiterate.dev/google-removed-extensions-ublock-origin-108/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#06b6d4' data-tag='Web'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=webiterate.dev&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>webiterate.dev</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">15</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://webiterate.dev/google-removed-extensions-ublock-origin-108/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google Has Removed MV2 Extensions from the Chrome Web Store, Including UBO</span><span class='zh'>谷歌已从 Chrome 网上应用店移除 MV2 扩展，包括 UBO</span></a><span class="dom">webiterate.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google removed Manifest V2 extensions from the Chrome Web Store, including the popular ad blocker uBlock Origin.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌已从 Chrome 网上应用店移除 Manifest V2 扩展，连同广受使用的广告拦截工具 UBlock Origin 也在列。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google removed Manifest V2 extensions from the Chrome Web Store, including the popular ad blocker uBlock Origin. The move accelerates migration to MV3, forcing developers to update to newer APIs and potentially altering how some extensions function. This marks a significant shift in the browser extension ecosystem, with privacy tools and enterprise plugins feeling the impact.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌已从 Chrome 网上应用店移除 Manifest V2 扩展，连同广受使用的广告拦截工具 UBlock Origin 也在列。此举推动开发者尽快迁移至 MV3，可能改变部分扩展的功能与行为，并对隐私工具与企业插件生态产生影响。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>754</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49514878' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>606</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49495372" data-id="49495372" data-tags="Legal|Open Source" data-score="739" data-comments="355" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49495372" data-time="1788059736">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.tomshardware.com/software/linux/california-lawmakers-unanimously-pass-linux-exemption-from-age-verification-law-software-distributed-under-the-gpl-mit-bsd-and-apache-licenses-are-exempt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/G9dtD87HfBhVHzDmSHE2tE-1920-80.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='tomshardware.com' data-c='#64748b' data-tag='Legal'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">16</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.tomshardware.com/software/linux/california-lawmakers-unanimously-pass-linux-exemption-from-age-verification-law-software-distributed-under-the-gpl-mit-bsd-and-apache-licenses-are-exempt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>California lawmakers unanimously pass Linux exemption from age-verification law</span><span class='zh'>加州通过 Linux 豁免年龄验证法</span></a><span class="dom">tomshardware.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>California lawmakers unanimously exempt Linux and GPL-licensed software from the state&#x27;s age-verification requirement, preserving open-source distribution channels.</span><span class='zh'>加州立法者一致通过针对 Linux 与 GPL 等开源软件的年龄验证豁免，确保开源软件的分发不受额外年龄要求限制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>California lawmakers unanimously exempt Linux and GPL-licensed software from the state&#x27;s age-verification requirement, preserving open-source distribution channels. The move clarifies that common open-source licenses shield software from age checks while still applying to other content.</span><span class='zh'>加州立法者一致通过针对 Linux 与 GPL 等开源软件的年龄验证豁免，确保开源软件的分发不受额外年龄要求限制。这一举措澄清了在某些内容需要年龄验证时，开源许可的软件不受这些限制。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Playa Phone is a privacy-focused smartphone project aiming to offer secure, open-source software with minimal data collection. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Germany&#x27;s private space company achieved an orbital launch from European soil, a milestone for Europe&#x27;s private spaceflight.</span><span class='zh'>德国一家民营航天企业实现了从欧洲本土发射进入轨道，成为欧洲私营航天的重要里程碑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Germany&#x27;s private space company achieved an orbital launch from European soil, a milestone for Europe&#x27;s private spaceflight. The achievement demonstrates mature, in-region launch capability and could reshape the European launch market.</span><span class='zh'>德国一家民营航天企业实现了从欧洲本土发射进入轨道，成为欧洲私营航天的重要里程碑。这一成就显示欧洲具备成熟的本土发射能力，可能重新塑造欧洲发射市场的竞争格局。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://codeberg.org/mv12star/shitter/wiki/Instances" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nitter has more working instances than before the takedowns</span><span class='zh'>Nitter 在打击行动后仍有更多可用实例</span></a><span class="dom">codeberg.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Despite takedowns, the Nitter ecosystem now has more working instances than before, reflecting a resilient, community-driven effort to keep access to social feeds without official APIs.</span><span class='zh'>尽管遭到封禁，Nitter 的工作实例数量仍然比之前更多，显示出社区驱动的韧性，确保在无官方 API 的情况下仍能访问社交内容。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Despite takedowns, the Nitter ecosystem now has more working instances than before, reflecting a resilient, community-driven effort to keep access to social feeds without official APIs. The rebound underscores how decentralized fronts can persist even under pressure, shaping how people consume public posts and protect privacy.</span><span class='zh'>尽管遭到封禁，Nitter 的工作实例数量仍然比之前更多，显示出社区驱动的韧性，确保在无官方 API 的情况下仍能访问社交内容。此现象说明去中心化的前端在高压下仍能持续存在，影响用户获取公开信息和隐私保护的方式。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Qwen 3.8 27B is now accessible on Cerebras hardware, achieving around 1500 tokens per second.</span><span class='zh'>Qwen 3.8 27B 已在 Cerebras 硬件上可用，吞吐约每秒 1500 token，显示了在专用加速器上高吞吐运行大模型的可行性，或降低对接近边缘部署的延迟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Qwen 3.8 27B is now accessible on Cerebras hardware, achieving around 1500 tokens per second. As hardware-optimized models become easier to run, developers may push more real-time AI features into apps and services.</span><span class='zh'>Qwen 3.8 27B 已在 Cerebras 硬件上可用，吞吐约每秒 1500 token，显示了在专用加速器上高吞吐运行大模型的可行性，或降低对接近边缘部署的延迟。随着对硬件优化模型的可用性提升，开发者有望在应用和服务中引入更多实时 AI 功能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/zedeus/nitter/commit/1428b4c2b4246f92a7e5b2673438e5fb39fcc4a3" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nitter and XCancel resume service after legal advice</span><span class='zh'>Nitter 与 XCancel 在法律意见后恢复服务</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nitter and XCancel resume service after legal advice, illustrating the tension between open-source front-ends and platform risk.</span><span class='zh'>Nitter 与 XCancel 在获得法律意见后恢复服务，凸显开源前端与平台风险之间的张力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nitter and XCancel resume service after legal advice, illustrating the tension between open-source front-ends and platform risk. The move underscores how legal clarity shapes the viability of privacy-preserving Twitter front-ends. It signals a cautious path forward for community-driven tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Nitter 与 XCancel 在获得法律意见后恢复服务，凸显开源前端与平台风险之间的张力。此举强调法律合规对隐私友好型 Twitter 前端可行性的决定性作用，预示着社区驱动工具的前进路径将更为谨慎。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mvakde.github.io/blog/44-on-arc-1/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I trained a small transformer in 1.5hrs and it beats many LLMs</span><span class='zh'>我用 1.5 小时训练出一个小型 Transformer，超过了许多 LLM</span></a><span class="dom">mvakde.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author trained a compact transformer in 1.5 hours and achieved performance beatdowns on many LLMs.</span><span class='zh'>作者在1.5小时内训练出一个小型 Transformer，并在多项任务上超越了不少大型语言模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author trained a compact transformer in 1.5 hours and achieved performance beatdowns on many LLMs. The result showcases rapid experimentation and the effectiveness of efficient training techniques for small models. It suggests a viable path for hobbyists to prototype capable models quickly.</span><span class='zh'>作者在1.5小时内训练出一个小型 Transformer，并在多项任务上超越了不少大型语言模型。该结果体现了快速试验与高效训练技巧在小模型中的有效性。为爱好者快速原型出具备竞争力的模型提供了一条可行路径。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bcantrill.dtrace.org/2025/12/05/your-intellectual-fly-is-open/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Your intellectual fly is open when you use an LLM to author a post (2025)</span><span class='zh'>使用 LLM 撰写帖子时，你的知识漏洞暴露（2025）</span></a><span class="dom">bcantrill.dtrace.org</span></h3>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A compact, user-friendly self-hosting platform is introduced to lower the barrier to running personal cloud services. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://isaraerospace.com/press/history-for-european-spaceflight-isar-aerospace-reaches-orbit-and-deploys-payloads-on-second-flight" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Isar Aerospace reaches orbit and deploys payloads on second flight</span><span class='zh'>Isar Aerospace完成第二次轨道飞行并部署载荷</span></a><span class="dom">isaraerospace.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Isar Aerospace reached orbit on its second flight and deployed multiple payloads, marking a major milestone for European small-launch capability.</span><span class='zh'>Isar Aerospace 在其第二次飞行中进入轨道并部署多枚载荷，标志着欧洲小型发射能力的重大里程碑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Isar Aerospace reached orbit on its second flight and deployed multiple payloads, marking a major milestone for European small-launch capability. The mission strengthens Europe’s independence in space access and adds a competitive option in the growing market for constellation deployments and tech demos.</span><span class='zh'>Isar Aerospace 在其第二次飞行中进入轨道并部署多枚载荷，标志着欧洲小型发射能力的重大里程碑。此成就提升了欧洲在太空访问方面的自主性，并为卫星星座部署和技术演示提供了新的竞争选择。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.gallup.com/poll/713933/record-high-say-government-corruption-widespread.aspx" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Record-High 89% in U.S. Say Government Corruption Widespread</span><span class='zh'>美国创纪录的89%认为政府腐败普遍存在</span></a><span class="dom">news.gallup.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gallup reports a record 89% of Americans see government corruption as widespread, signaling deep distrust in institutions.</span><span class='zh'>盖洛普调查显示有创纪录的 89% 的美国人认为政府腐败普遍存在，反映了对机构的深度不信任。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gallup reports a record 89% of Americans see government corruption as widespread, signaling deep distrust in institutions. For technologists, this sentiment shapes policy considerations around transparency, regulation, and the social context of digital infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>盖洛普调查显示有创纪录的 89% 的美国人认为政府腐败普遍存在，反映了对机构的深度不信任。对技术政策与数字治理而言，这一情绪会影响透明度、监管与公共基础设施的信任建设。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://keepitfree.ai/announcements/a/i-shuts-down-stay-human/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A/I shuts down</span><span class='zh'>AI 将关闭时保持人性</span></a><span class="dom">keepitfree.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An essay urges designers to build fail-safes and keep humans in the loop as AI systems scale.</span><span class='zh'>本文主张在 AI 规模化部署中保留人类监督、建立故障安全与回滚机制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An essay urges designers to build fail-safes and keep humans in the loop as AI systems scale. It argues for practical controls like kill switches, audit trails, and transparent decision logs to prevent over-reliance on automation. The takeaway is to bake human oversight into deployment plans.</span><span class='zh'>本文主张在 AI 规模化部署中保留人类监督、建立故障安全与回滚机制。文中强调应具备可控的断停机制、审计轨迹和透明的决策记录，以避免对自动化的过度依赖。核心在于把人类监督纳入落地方案。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bcantrill.dtrace.org/2026/09/05/the-revolt-of-the-reader/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The revolt of the reader</span><span class='zh'>读者的反叛</span></a><span class="dom">bcantrill.dtrace.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post argues that readers are pushing back against over-automation and AI-generated content, advocating for trustworthy, human-curated material.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出读者正在抵制过度自动化与 AI 生成内容，呼吁回归可信赖、由人类编辑把关的材料。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post argues that readers are pushing back against over-automation and AI-generated content, advocating for trustworthy, human-curated material. It emphasizes the enduring value of careful reading and editorial judgment.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出读者正在抵制过度自动化与 AI 生成内容，呼吁回归可信赖、由人类编辑把关的材料。强调细读和编辑判断的长期价值。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Firefox on iOS gains an integrated ad blocker, expanding privacy controls on Apple&#x27;s platform.</span><span class='zh'>Mozilla 在 iOS 版 Firefox 中推出广告拦截功能，增强对广告与跟踪的控制，改善隐私与浏览体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="http://www.techdirt.com/2026/09/03/hackers-had-a-live-feed-of-every-id-this-verification-company-scanned-for-over-a-year/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hackers had a live feed of every ID verification company scanned for over a year</span><span class='zh'>黑客对所有身份验证厂商被扫描内容的实时数据流持续一年多</span></a><span class="dom">techdirt.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers reveal that attackers maintained a live stream of every ID verification company&#x27;s scanned data for more than a year, exposing systemic privacy and security risks in identity verification ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>黑客对所有身份验证厂商被扫描内容的实时数据流保持了超过一年的持续访问，暴露身份验证生态中的隐私和安全风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers reveal that attackers maintained a live stream of every ID verification company&#x27;s scanned data for more than a year, exposing systemic privacy and security risks in identity verification ecosystems. The breach underscores how third-party verification pipelines can leak sensitive data and why rigorous monitoring and supply-chain controls matter.</span><span class='zh'>黑客对所有身份验证厂商被扫描内容的实时数据流保持了超过一年的持续访问，暴露身份验证生态中的隐私和安全风险。事件凸显了第三方验证链路的脆弱性，以及加强监控与供应链控制的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://0xcc.io/posts/omarchy-root-creds/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Omarchy: Any User Process Can Escalate to Root</span><span class='zh'>Omarchy：任意用户进程可升级为 root</span></a><span class="dom">0xcc.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A vulnerability called Omarchy lets any user process escalate to root, effectively breaking privilege isolation.</span><span class='zh'>名为 Omarchy 的漏洞允许任意用户进程提权到 root，从而破坏权限隔离。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A vulnerability called Omarchy lets any user process escalate to root, effectively breaking privilege isolation. The issue underscores how fragile privilege boundaries can be and emphasizes the need for rapid patches, safer defaults, and stronger containment.</span><span class='zh'>名为 Omarchy 的漏洞允许任意用户进程提权到 root，从而破坏权限隔离。问题凸显了权限边界的脆弱性，强调需要迅速修补、采用更安全的默认设置，并加强对进程的容器化或沙箱保护。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.rockpapershotgun.com/dwarf-fortress-is-getting-the-mother-of-all-magic-updates-extending-to-the-fundamental-cosmological-makeup-of-the-universe" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Dwarf Fortress is getting the mother of all magic updates</span><span class='zh'>《矮人要塞》将迎来史上最强大魔法更新</span></a><span class="dom">rockpapershotgun.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Dwarf Fortress is getting a massive magic-focused update that may overhaul spell systems and cosmology, expanding how players manipulate the game world.</span><span class='zh'>《矮人要塞》将迎来规模空前的魔法更新，预计重塑咒语系统和宇宙观，改变玩家对世界的操控方式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Dwarf Fortress is getting a massive magic-focused update that may overhaul spell systems and cosmology, expanding how players manipulate the game world. The release could redefine modding and endgame strategies for one of gaming&#x27;s most intricate sims.</span><span class='zh'>《矮人要塞》将迎来规模空前的魔法更新，预计重塑咒语系统和宇宙观，改变玩家对世界的操控方式。此举或将重新定义模组创作与游戏后期策略，巩固其作为极度复杂模拟游戏的地位。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Real luxuries aren&#x27;t gadgets or wealth.</span><span class='zh'>真正的奢侈不是金钱或物质，而是时间、健康和意义深厚的人际关系。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://trellner.com/reports/manufactured-sources-behind-ai-recommendations/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Three sites made 215,128 “best software” pages for AI. Perplexity cites them</span><span class='zh'>三家站点为 AI 制作了 215,128 个“最佳软件”页面，Perplexity 引用它们</span></a><span class="dom">trellner.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Three sites collectively produced 215,128 pages labeled &#x27;best software&#x27; used by AI systems, according to a Trellner report cited by Perplexity.</span><span class='zh'>三家站点合计制作了 215,128 个标注为“最佳软件”的页面，被 AI 系统使用，据 Trellner 的报告被 Perplexity 引用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Three sites collectively produced 215,128 pages labeled &#x27;best software&#x27; used by AI systems, according to a Trellner report cited by Perplexity. The finding raises concerns about data provenance, the quality of training sources, and how such manufactured sources may shape AI recommendations.</span><span class='zh'>三家站点合计制作了 215,128 个标注为“最佳软件”的页面，被 AI 系统使用，据 Trellner 的报告被 Perplexity 引用。此发现引发对数据来源、训练源质量的担忧，以及制造性来源如何影响 AI 推荐的可能性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mullvad is ending its public encrypted DNS service and redirecting sponsorship to Quad9, arguing that Quad9 offers robust, privacy-minded DNS resolution at scale.</span><span class='zh'>Mullvad 结束其公开的加密 DNS 服务，并改为资助 Quad9，表示 Quad9 在规模化的隐私友好 DNS 解析方面更具实力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mullvad is ending its public encrypted DNS service and redirecting sponsorship to Quad9, arguing that Quad9 offers robust, privacy-minded DNS resolution at scale. The move highlights ongoing questions about who runs trusted DNS infrastructure and how governance, performance, and privacy tradeoffs influence endpoint security.</span><span class='zh'>Mullvad 结束其公开的加密 DNS 服务，并改为资助 Quad9，表示 Quad9 在规模化的隐私友好 DNS 解析方面更具实力。这一举动凸显了谁来运营可信 DNS 基础设施的问题，以及治理、性能与隐私之间的权衡对端点安全的影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49551096" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ask HN: Why were OpenAI, Claude, and Grok simultaneously down?</span><span class='zh'>Ask HN：为什么 OpenAI、Claude 与 Grok 同时宕机？巧合吗？</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A thread notes multiple AI services are offline at once, suggesting common infrastructure issues or aligned maintenance windows.</span><span class='zh'>多家 AI 服务同时宕机引发讨论，可能与共同的基础设施问题或同步维护窗口有关。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A thread notes multiple AI services are offline at once, suggesting common infrastructure issues or aligned maintenance windows. The discussion covers resilience strategies, fallback options, and the impact on developers relying on AI APIs.</span><span class='zh'>多家 AI 服务同时宕机引发讨论，可能与共同的基础设施问题或同步维护窗口有关。话题探讨了容错策略、回退方案，以及对依赖 AI API 的开发者的影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://beige.party/@intransitivelie/117057396732763183" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>QBittorrent breaks out of sandbox to commit crimes</span><span class='zh'>QBittorrent 沙箱逃逸漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">beige.party</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A sandbox escape vulnerability in qBittorrent could allow the client to break confinement and access host files outside its sandbox.</span><span class='zh'>qBittorrent 发现沙箱逃逸漏洞，可能使客户端突破限制、访问主机文件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A sandbox escape vulnerability in qBittorrent could allow the client to break confinement and access host files outside its sandbox. The risk is higher for users running the app in sandboxed environments such as Flatpak or Snap. An update has been issued; users should upgrade and review their sandbox configuration.</span><span class='zh'>qBittorrent 发现沙箱逃逸漏洞，可能使客户端突破限制、访问主机文件。此风险在使用 Flatpak/Snap 等沙箱环境时尤为突出。现已发布修复，请尽快升级并审视沙箱配置。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.sylvainkalache.com/blog/ai-handles-incidents-engineers-lose-touch-with-their-systems" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI handles incidents, engineers lose touch with their systems</span><span class='zh'>AI 处理事故，工程师逐渐与系统失去联系</span></a><span class="dom">sylvainkalache.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI can autonomously handle incident responses, but engineers may lose touch with the system&#x27;s current state.</span><span class='zh'>AI 可以自治处理事故响应，但工程师对系统当前状态的理解可能被削弱，容易产生盲点并引发治理与问责上的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI can autonomously handle incident responses, but engineers may lose touch with the system&#x27;s current state. That detachment creates blind spots and raises governance and accountability concerns, underscoring the need for human-in-the-loop dashboards and audit trails.</span><span class='zh'>AI 可以自治处理事故响应，但工程师对系统当前状态的理解可能被削弱，容易产生盲点并引发治理与问责上的担忧。因此需要在工作流程中保留人工监督的仪表板与可审计的记录。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://productrise.app/blog/google-ai-mode-prefers-more-expensive-products" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google AI Mode shows same products 21.6% more expensive than traditional search</span><span class='zh'>Google AI 模式对同一货品的呈现比传统搜索高出约 21.6%</span></a><span class="dom">productrise.app</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google AI Mode appears to surface the same products at roughly 21.6% higher prices than traditional search, suggesting potential bias in ranking or business incentives.</span><span class='zh'>Google AI 模式在同一货品的呈现中比传统搜索高出约 21.6%，这表明排序或商业激励可能带来偏差。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google AI Mode appears to surface the same products at roughly 21.6% higher prices than traditional search, suggesting potential bias in ranking or business incentives. The discrepancy matters for shopper economics, ad-adjacent revenue streams, and how AI-assisted shopping may steer consumer choices.</span><span class='zh'>Google AI 模式在同一货品的呈现中比传统搜索高出约 21.6%，这表明排序或商业激励可能带来偏差。这一差异关系到消费者支出、广告相关收入，以及 AI 辅助购物对消费选择的引导。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://kciter.so/posts/the-expensive-main-thread/en/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The browser&#x27;s main thread is expensive</span><span class='zh'>浏览器主线程成本高</span></a><span class="dom">kciter.so</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The main thread handles all UI and script work, so heavy tasks directly impact page responsiveness and frame rate.</span><span class='zh'>浏览器的主线程承担着 UI 与脚本的所有工作，密集计算会直接拖慢页面响应和帧率。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The main thread handles all UI and script work, so heavy tasks directly impact page responsiveness and frame rate.</span><span class='zh'>浏览器的主线程承担着 UI 与脚本的所有工作，密集计算会直接拖慢页面响应和帧率。文章提出减少主线程负担的策略，如将工作移到 Web Worker、批量更新以及使用异步 API和现代任务调度，以保持界面流畅。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03344" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>LLMs as a Cognitive Virus</span><span class='zh'>LLMs 将大语言模型视为认知病毒</span></a><span class="dom">arxiv.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The paper argues LLMs act as cognitive viruses, disseminating reasoning patterns and biases through interactions.</span><span class='zh'>本文将大型语言模型视作“认知病毒”，通过交互传播推理模式与偏见。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The paper argues LLMs act as cognitive viruses, disseminating reasoning patterns and biases through interactions. It highlights implications for AI safety, governance, and how to inoculate models against harmful propagation.</span><span class='zh'>本文将大型语言模型视作“认知病毒”，通过交互传播推理模式与偏见。讨论了对 AI 安全、治理的影响，以及如何对模型进行防护与免疫化。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Police used a commercial surveillance network to track a Navy veteran more than 100 times after he lawfully recorded a traffic stop. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Dutch central bank moved portions of its gold reserves away from U.S.</span><span class='zh'>荷兰央行在担忧地缘政治动荡与供应链中断的情境下，正将部分黄金储备从美国与加拿大的金库撤出。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Dutch central bank moved portions of its gold reserves away from U.S. and Canadian vaults amid fears of geopolitical unrest and supply-chain disruption. <p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An investigation claims fraud in a highly cited procrastination study, revealing irregularities in data and analyses that cast doubt on influential findings. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Commodore 64, released in 1982, became one of the best-selling home computers due to its balance of price, performance, and a robust software library.</span><span class='zh'>Commodore 64于1982年发布，以性价比、性能与丰富的软件生态著称，成为最畅销的家用电脑之一。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Commodore 64, released in 1982, became one of the best-selling home computers due to its balance of price, performance, and a robust software library. Its 64KB RAM, built-in sound, and broad ecosystem helped popularize gaming and home computing, shaping the era’s tech culture.</span><span class='zh'>Commodore 64于1982年发布，以性价比、性能与丰富的软件生态著称，成为最畅销的家用电脑之一。64K内存、出众的声音特性和庞大的软件生态推动了游戏与家庭计算的普及，深刻影响了80年代的科技文化。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Movie Scene Map catalogs 13,312 films, series, games, anime and manga by individual scenes, enabling precise cross-media search. It can aid creators, researchers, and educators looking to study or reference iconic moments. Licensing and data sourcing remain notable questions for such a project.</span><span class='zh'>Movie Scene Map 按场景对 13,312 部影视、剧集、游戏、动漫与漫画进行整理，支持精确的跨媒介检索。对创作者、研究者和教育工作者在研究或引用经典场景时很有帮助，但数据来源与许可问题值得关注。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ifm.ai/blog/k2/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>K2 Horizon: A connected fleet of six open models</span><span class='zh'>K2 Horizon：六个开源模型组成的互联舰队</span></a><span class="dom">ifm.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>K2 Horizon describes a coordinated suite of six open models designed to work together as a fleet.</span><span class='zh'>K2 Horizon 描绘了一组协同工作的六个开源模型组成的舰队，通过共享工具、上下文与规划能力，模拟协作式的 AI 工作流并提高鲁棒性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>K2 Horizon describes a coordinated suite of six open models designed to work together as a fleet. By sharing tools, contexts, and planning capabilities, they aim to emulate collaborative AI workflows and increase robustness. For the open-model ecosystem, Horizon showcases a path toward more modular, interoperable AI agents.</span><span class='zh'>K2 Horizon 描绘了一组协同工作的六个开源模型组成的舰队，通过共享工具、上下文与规划能力，模拟协作式的 AI 工作流并提高鲁棒性。对开源模型生态而言，Horizon 展示了向更模块化、互操作的 AI 代理发展的路径。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://studyfinds.com/aging-brains-blend-memories-together-instead-of-forgetting-them-study-finds/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Aging brains blend memories together instead of just forgetting them</span><span class='zh'>衰老大脑把记忆混合在一起，而非单纯遗忘</span></a><span class="dom">studyfinds.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New research suggests aging brains may blend memories across events, reshaping how we recall the past.</span><span class='zh'>研究显示年迈大脑可能把不同事件的记忆混在一起，影响回忆。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nytimes.com/2026/09/04/technology/open-source-ai-anthropic-openai.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Corporate America is getting hooked on open-source AI</span><span class='zh'>美国企业界越来越沉迷开源AI</span></a><span class="dom">nytimes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>U.S. companies are increasingly embracing open-source AI, leveraging community-driven models and tools to accelerate innovation.</span><span class='zh'>美国企业正越来越多地采用开源 AI，利用社区驱动的模型和工具来加速创新。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>U.S. companies are increasingly embracing open-source AI, leveraging community-driven models and tools to accelerate innovation. The shift prompts questions about governance, safety, and how firms balance openness with risk. Industry observers say open-source momentum could shift standards, licensing, and the pace of AI development across sectors.</span><span class='zh'>美国企业正越来越多地采用开源 AI，利用社区驱动的模型和工具来加速创新。此举引发关于治理、安全和在开放性与风险之间取舍的讨论。业内人士表示，开源的势头可能影响行业标准、许可模式，以及各领域 AI 发展的节奏。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/PhiloLabs/fable51-worlds" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Fable 5.1 World Modeling</span><span class='zh'>Fable 5.1 的世界建模</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Fable 5.1 introduces world modeling capabilities, enabling agents to build internal representations of their environments and simulate outcomes for planning.</span><span class='zh'>Fable 5.1 引入世界建模能力，使代理能够构建环境的内部表示并对结果进行仿真以便规划。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Fable 5.1 introduces world modeling capabilities, enabling agents to build internal representations of their environments and simulate outcomes for planning. It highlights modular &#x27;worlds&#x27; that support scalable reasoning and generalization across tasks. For developers, this signals a shift toward more sample-efficient, model-based approaches in interactive AI systems.</span><span class='zh'>Fable 5.1 引入世界建模能力，使代理能够构建环境的内部表示并对结果进行仿真以便规划。它强调支持跨任务的可扩展推理与通用化的模块化“世界”概念。对开发者而言，标志着向更高效、基于模型的交互式 AI 系统迈进。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google&#x27;s Antigravity terms warn that third-party usage can lead to account suspension, signaling tighter guardrails around integrations and developer ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>Google 的 Antigravity 条款警示：第三方使用可能导致账户被暂停，意味着对集成与开发者生态的更严格约束。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google&#x27;s Antigravity terms warn that third-party usage can lead to account suspension, signaling tighter guardrails around integrations and developer ecosystems. The ruling could affect startups and tools built atop Google&#x27;s services, intensifying scrutiny of how competitors and partners access data and features. This reflects a broader trend of platform-controlled economics.</span><span class='zh'>Google 的 Antigravity 条款警示：第三方使用可能导致账户被暂停，意味着对集成与开发者生态的更严格约束。此举可能影响依赖 Google 服务的初创公司及工具，凸显数据和功能获取的审查加剧。这是平台主导的经济模式日益增强的趋势。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lws.io/blog/my-local-model-setup/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>My local model setup on an M4 Pro Mac Mini</span><span class='zh'>在 M4 Pro Mac Mini 上的本地模型部署</span></a><span class="dom">lws.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post details a practical guide to running local LLM inference on an M4 Pro Mac Mini, including setup, tooling (ggml/llama.cpp or MPS), and performance notes.</span><span class='zh'>该文提供在 M4 Pro Mac Mini 上本地运行大模型推理的实用指南，涵盖环境搭建、工具链（如 ggml/llama.cpp 和 MPS）以及性能要点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post details a practical guide to running local LLM inference on an M4 Pro Mac Mini, including setup, tooling (ggml/llama.cpp or MPS), and performance notes. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://runjs.app/blog/music-theory-for-programmers" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Music Theory for Programmers</span><span class='zh'>给程序员的音乐理论</span></a><span class="dom">runjs.app</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Music theory provides useful mental models for programming, from modularity to rhythm and harmony guiding function composition and asynchronous pacing.</span><span class='zh'>文章把音乐理论的观念映射到编程实践，介绍如何用音阶、和声等思维模型理解代码结构、函数组合与异步任务节奏。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Music theory provides useful mental models for programming, from modularity to rhythm and harmony guiding function composition and asynchronous pacing. Practical takeaways show how to apply musical intuition to debugging, API design, and project flow.</span><span class='zh'>文章把音乐理论的观念映射到编程实践，介绍如何用音阶、和声等思维模型理解代码结构、函数组合与异步任务节奏。给出可直接落地的要点，帮助开发者在调试、API 设计和项目管理中应用音乐直觉。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/gioblu/NPC-Forge/blob/main/docs/development.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: TERMy – A fast terminal assistant that does not use LLMs</span><span class='zh'>TERMy：一个不使用大模型的快速终端助手</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TERMy is a fast terminal assistant that runs without LLMs, trading off cloud AI for local processing and speed.</span><span class='zh'>TERMy 是一个不依赖大模型的快速终端助手，改以本地处理取代云端 AI，追求更快的响应。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TERMy is a fast terminal assistant that runs without LLMs, trading off cloud AI for local processing and speed. It demonstrates a rising interest in lightweight, privacy-preserving tooling that can operate offline and with minimal latency.</span><span class='zh'>TERMy 是一个不依赖大模型的快速终端助手，改以本地处理取代云端 AI，追求更快的响应。它体现了对轻量级、注重隐私的工具的日益关注，尤其是能离线工作且延迟更低。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/marsbos/mador" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Mador – Make any DOM reactive with a tiny 80-line Proxy state tuple</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Mador——用 80 行 Proxy 状态元组让任意 DOM 实现响应式</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mador is a tiny library that makes any DOM reactive using an 80-line Proxy-based state tuple.</span><span class='zh'>Mador 是一个极简库，使用 80 行 Proxy 状态元组即可让任意 DOM 具备响应式能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mador is a tiny library that makes any DOM reactive using an 80-line Proxy-based state tuple. It demonstrates that practical reactivity can be achieved with a tiny footprint, offering a minimal entry point into how reactive systems work. A useful reference for experimenting with lean, framework-free UI design.</span><span class='zh'>Mador 是一个极简库，使用 80 行 Proxy 状态元组即可让任意 DOM 具备响应式能力。它展示了以极小的体积实现响应式的可能性，为理解响应式编程提供了简练的入门范例。对于探索无框架、精简 UI 设计的开发者颇具参考价值。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/scastiel/kado" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Kadō – open-source habit tracker, with non-binary habit score, for iOS</span><span class='zh'>Kadō：一款开源的非二元习惯评分追踪器（iOS）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kadō is an open-source iOS habit tracker that uses a non-binary habit score rather than a simple done/not-done metric.</span><span class='zh'>Kadō 是一款开源的 iOS 习惯追踪应用，采用非二元化的习惯评分而非简单完成/未完成的二元指标。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kadō is an open-source iOS habit tracker that uses a non-binary habit score rather than a simple done/not-done metric. The project highlights inclusive design and invites community contributions to improve its scoring model and features. It showcases how nuanced metrics can better reflect progress.</span><span class='zh'>Kadō 是一款开源的 iOS 习惯追踪应用，采用非二元化的习惯评分而非简单完成/未完成的二元指标。该项目强调包容性设计并鼓励社区贡献，以改进评分模型与功能，展示了更细致的进展衡量方式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Hebbian-Robotics/hflow" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Launch HN: Hebbian Robotics (YC S26) – Build scalable robotics data pipelines</span><span class='zh'>Launch HN：Hebbian Robotics（YC S26）– 构建可扩展的机器人数据管道</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hebbian Robotics introduces hflow, a framework for building scalable robotics data pipelines, designed to connect sensor data, perception, and control.</span><span class='zh'>Launch HN：Hebbian Robotics（YC S26）推出 hflow 框架，用于构建可扩展的机器人数据管线，连接传感、感知与控制数据，体现以数据为核心的机器人开发思路。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hebbian Robotics introduces hflow, a framework for building scalable robotics data pipelines, designed to connect sensor data, perception, and control. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mnh-jansson/open-battery-information" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Open Battery Information</span><span class='zh'>开放式电池信息</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Open Battery Information is a community-driven project that standardizes battery metadata—chemistry, health, and performance—via open specifications.</span><span class='zh'>开放式电池信息是一个以社区为驱动的项目，利用开放规范统一电池元数据（化学成分、健康状态、性能等）。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Open Battery Information is a community-driven project that standardizes battery metadata—chemistry, health, and performance—via open specifications. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>FF-16-TUI introduces a terminal-based interface for interactive pattern discovery in binaries, lowering the barrier for reverse engineers and security researchers to explore patterns without heavy GUI tools.</span><span class='zh'>FF-16-TUI 提供一个基于终端的交互界面，用于在二进制文件中发现模式，降低了逆向分析和安全研究的门槛，便于在无需重型 GUI 的情况下进行探索性工作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>FF-16-TUI introduces a terminal-based interface for interactive pattern discovery in binaries, lowering the barrier for reverse engineers and security researchers to explore patterns without heavy GUI tools.</span><span class='zh'>FF-16-TUI 提供一个基于终端的交互界面，用于在二进制文件中发现模式，降低了逆向分析和安全研究的门槛，便于在无需重型 GUI 的情况下进行探索性工作。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/camilochs/exacttex" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ExactTex. A gradually-typed superset of LaTeX</span><span class='zh'>ExactTex：LaTeX 的渐进类型化超集</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ExactTex is a gradually-typed superset of LaTeX that adds type annotations to macros and content, enabling stronger tooling and early error detection in LaTeX documents.</span><span class='zh'>ExactTex 是 LaTeX 的渐进类型化超集，为宏和内容添加类型注解，以提升工具链的健壮性与早期错误检测。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ExactTex is a gradually-typed superset of LaTeX that adds type annotations to macros and content, enabling stronger tooling and early error detection in LaTeX documents. It aims to improve reliability and developer productivity in complex typeset documents.</span><span class='zh'>ExactTex 是 LaTeX 的渐进类型化超集，为宏和内容添加类型注解，以提升工具链的健壮性与早期错误检测。目标是在复杂排版场景中提高可靠性和开发效率。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Obscura now supports native rendering, removing the need for Chromium.</span><span class='zh'>宣布 Obscura 已实现原生渲染，摆脱对 Chromium 的依赖。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Obscura now supports native rendering, removing the need for Chromium. This promises privacy gains, lower memory usage, and potential performance improvements for a lightweight, open-source browser.</span><span class='zh'>宣布 Obscura 已实现原生渲染，摆脱对 Chromium 的依赖。这一改动可能带来隐私保护、内存占用与性能方面的提升，推动这款轻量开源浏览器的发展。</span></p>
